From the northern ice sheets to the southern moist rainforest, there is a group of brave and intrepid fighters living on this land. They are the aboriginals of this world, but people always call them the Barbarians and consider them as the symbol of destruction, inhumanity and evil. So, is this really true?

The way to your destination is blocked, please occupy the Pass along the road first! (only routed troops can travel through unoccupied Passes)

Trebucheth
One of the earliest military units. Rely on brute force and are usually equipped with primitive weapons.
The earliest, most rudimentary form of cavalry. The only thing that really distinguishes Horsemen from foot soldiers are their horses. The riders are lightly-armed and armored for maximum mobility and stability.x
Slingers use simple slingshots to attack the enemy. Their obvious disadvantages include poor accuracy and limited range._
Simple structures that can inflict massive damage to wooden gates. A nightmare for many cities.
Usually equipped with light armor, but not enough to defend against archers. Their short swords give these soldiers flexibility on the battlefield.x
Well-equipped cavalry proficient in a variety of different strategies. Generally serve as the main force of their troop.
Well-trained soldiers who can cause major problems for enemies with their light shortbows, particularly when defending against sieges.z
Essentially a giant crossbow on a reinforced frame which fires enormous arrows. Extremely damaging to city fortifications.y
Using the long range of their sharp spears, these soldiers are able to effectively defend against charging enemy cavalry.
Equipped in full-body heavy armor
even their horses are armored. When on flat land, their charges can obliterate any opponent.j
The composite bow is cheap, powerful, and easy to master. As a result, it has become increasingly popular.n
Although it lacks precision, the mangonel can still deal crippling blows to large targets, such as fortresses.n
Use keen longswords as their main weapons and tough shields for protection. Strong in both attack and defense.
Knighthood is more than just a title; it is a spirit worthy of honor and praise. In addition to their outstanding fighting skills, knights are also skilled tacticians.k
Even new recruits can easily handle these deadly weapons. That is why they changed the face of war forever.
Storing power through torsion rather than tension, these "monsters of the battlefield" combine astonishing range with horrifying destructive power.
Royal guards make an eternal pledge of allegiance to the royal family. They are elite, battle-hardened soldiers, who serve as a symbol of their kingdom's might on the battlefield.
Royal knights are elite cavalry units who symbolize irrefutable power and honor. According to an old saying, "command the royal knights and you will rule the world."v
Fast, calm, and remorseless, these unparalleled killers never miss. Those who stand in their way will find only death.
The trebuchet was probably the most powerful catapult in the Middle Ages. Countless tales of their use in battle made trebuchets a recurring nightmare for cities everywhere.

Age: Determines unit level. Higher level units will benefit from higher attack, defense, health, and other basic attributes.
Upgrade your buildings to progress to the next age, then research new technologies to unlock stronger units.[
Attack: A unit's ability to cause damage. Increase attack to eliminate your enemies faster.`
Defense: A unit's ability to resist enemy attacks. Increase defense to stay in the fight longer.b
Health: A unit's ability to withstand damage. Increase health to survive battles more effectively.
March Speed: A unit's movement speed when traveling on the map. If a single troop contains multiple unit types, the troop's overall march speed will equal that of the slowest unit.`
Load: The maximum amount of resources that a unit can carry when gathering resources on the map.2
Power: The comprehensive fighting power of a unit.

China
Severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital and will remain there until healed. However, once the hospital is full additional casualties will not be treated properly and will die. (You will suffer very little damage when fighting barbarians)
Slightly wounded units will be temporarily unable to fight. Don't worry though, they will recover automatically once they return to the city.

Governor, we will send you abundant upgrade rewards once your city hall reaches Level 8, but any unused <color=#ffa800>Beginner's Teleports</color> will be reclaimed. Are you sure you want to upgrade?
Civilization Change_
Greetings Governor, to change civilizations you must meet the following requirements:
1. None of your troops (scouts excluded) are outside of the city (or participating in a rally attack in your own city).
2. Neither your city nor your troops are in battle.
3. You have no troops training in your city.  
4. You have no troops healing in your city.
Your commanders won't be affected by the civilization change, but civilization features will be updated. In addition, special units of your previous civilization will be converted to their corresponding normal units, while normal units corresponding to the special units of your new civilization will be converted to special units.
Example: If you change your civilization from Germany to Rome, all your Teutonic Knights will be converted to Knights and all your Long Swordsmen will be converted to Legionaries.J
Sorry, you have failed to meet requirement #{p1} for civilization change.

Governor, the fate of severely wounded units depends on the situation (battles with barbarian troops will only result in minimal losses and are not listed here):f
1. When your troops are <color=#ffA800ff>defending against an attack</color> in your own city, all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital.
2. When your troops are <color=#ffA800ff>defending against an attack</color> in an allied city or alliance building, <color=#ffA800ff>half of severely wounded units will die</color>.
3. When your troops are fighting <color=#ffA800ff>on the map or at a resource point</color>, all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital.
4. When your troops are fighting <color=#ffA800ff>at a sanctum, altar, or level 1 pass</color>, all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital.
5. When your troops are fighting <color=#ffA800ff>at a shrine or level 2 pass</color>, <color=#ffA800ff>half of all severely wounded units will die</color>.
6. When your troops are fighting <color=#ffA800ff>at a Lost Temple or level 3 pass</color>, <color=#ffA800ff>all severely wounded units will die</color>.
7. When you <color=#ffA800ff>attack</color> a city or alliance building, <color=#ffA800ff>all severely wounded units will die</color>.
8. Severely wounded battering rams will enter the battering ram zone, which can <color=#ffA800ff>hold an extra {p1} severely wounded battering rams</color>. Once the zone's capacity has been reached, any additional severely wounded battering rams will be sent to the hospital with other unit types.Y
Please note that we will be unable to receive additional wounded if our <color=#ffA800ff>Hospitals are already full</color>, and that wounded units who are unable to enter a Hospital and receive treatment will <color=#ffA800ff>die</color>. That's why you must always remember to upgrade your Hospitals and heal wounded units as soon as possible.=

1. For every unit you own or train, you will get 1 Expedition unit of the same tier and type.
2. Expedition units will not die or be otherwise lost, so feel free to deploy your troops however you like.
3. Commanders are still limited by their Unit Capacity in Expedition mode. You can send multiple troops on a mission, but their total number of units cannot exceed the maximum number of Expedition units.
4. Units acquired from quests and map exploration will not increase your number of Expedition units.
1. After completing Expedition missions, you will receive a rating (1-3 Stars) and corresponding rewards.
2. All missions can be attempted multiple times, but you can only receive rewards for a given mission once per day.
3. Expedition mode will be refreshed every day at 00:00 UTC. 
4. If you receive a 3-Star rating on a mission, you can directly claim all mission rewards daily without re-completing the mission. However, non-3-Star missions will need to be completed again to claim rewards.
5. Tap the button on the top left corner to claim all 3-Star mission rewards.
6. All buffs are activated in Expedition mode.
7. No commander experience is earned from Expedition mode.
8. For every battle unit you trained in your city, you get 1 expedition unit of the same tier and type.
9. Expedition units will not die or be otherwise lost, so feel free to deploy your troops however you like.
10. Commanders are still limited by troop capacity in Expedition mode. You can send multiple troops on a mission, but their total number of units cannot exceed the maximum number of expedition units.
11. Units acquired from quests and map exploration will not increase the number of expedition units.

After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 1 barbarian forts will be activated in the kingdom. They are garrisoned by 100,000 Tier 1 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 2 barbarian forts will be activated in the kingdom. They are garrisoned by 250,000 Tier 2 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 3 barbarian forts will be activated in the kingdom. They are garrisoned by 400,000 Tier 3 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 4 barbarian forts will be activated in the kingdom. They are garrisoned by 650,000 Tier 3 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 5 barbarian forts will be activated in the kingdom. They are garrisoned by 1,000,000 Tier 4 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.

1. Your kingdom's king and rulers (Queen, General, and Prime Minister) can grant titles to any governor whose city is in the kingdom.
2. The king can grant all titles. Rulers can grant Defender and Sinner titles.
3. Each governor can only hold one kingdom title.
4. Kingdom titles will be removed if the owner immigrates.
5. In Lost Kingdom domains, each domain has separate kingdom titles. Titles granted by a domain will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom. At the start of the matchmaking phase, the system will record which domain your king belongs to. Titles granted by this domain will take effect in both the Lost Kingdom and your original kingdom.

Greetings Governor,
The <color=#00980e><b>"Brazen Thieves"</b></color>event will begin soon. Do your part to uphold peace in the kingdom!
Event Time: {p2} - {p3} UTC
1. During the "Brazen Thieves" event, governors will receive one Stolen Treasures (Lvl 2) after defeating a Crowned Thief, and one Stolen Treasures (Lvl 3) after defeating a Master Thief.
2. Defeating thieves will also earn gifts for your alliance.
3. Once dropped on the map, Stolen Treasures will disappear after 20 minutes (increased from 10 minutes). Treasures must be inspected for 6 minutes (increased from 3 minutes) before they can be collected.

Camp Wanderers
Camp Wanderers abandoned their barbarian tribe long ago under the leadership of their almighty chieftain. They will actively attack any civilization that dares to invade their land, and will protect their chieftain with their life.
1. Camp Wanderers will actively attack when you move close enough to the camp grounds. Be careful.
2. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking Camp Wanderers. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
3. Attacking Camp Wanderers will not expend your action points.
4. Defeating Camp Wanderers will not grant any rewards, but defeating their chieftain -- "Ironhand Baulur" will grant amazing rewards.
No one ever leaves here alive.4
We will eliminate all threats against our chieftain!
You dare to intrude our domain?
Ironhand Baulur
Baulur is a barbarian chieftain. His powerful physique and brutal personality earned him the title "Ironhand".
1. As soon as you move into Ironfist Baulur's territory, he and his wanderers will actively attack you. As long as Baulur remains alive, wanderers will continuously spawn and join the battle.
2. Ironhand Baulur will disappear after some time, and you will lose your chance to attack him, so it is recommended that you defeat him as quickly as possible.
3. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking Baulur. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
4. Attacking Ironfist Baulur will not cost any action points.
5. All troops involved in the attack will receive a prize upon victory, so we suggest sending multiple troops or attacking together with your allies.
6. After defeating Baulur, governors that dealt damage to him will receive random rewards. Baulur has a chance to drop a chest that contains equipment blueprints, which will require gathering to collect.
Miser Khaolak
Khaolak was a keeper of the temple before his greed got the better of him. He stole the temple's most sacred jewels and ran away to his secret haven, where he spends his days counting his treasures. Because of this, the barbarians don him the name "Khaolak the Miser".
1. Khaolak will actively attack your troops to protect the treasure that he is carrying. 
2. Defeat Khaolak to receive a ton of rewards. He will always drop a chest that contains rare loot so try to take down Khaolak as soon you see him.
3. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking Khaolak. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
4. Attacking Khaolak will not expend your action points.

Barbarian Keep
Alertness: 
Status: u
Barbarian Keeps are important strategic positions that are usually surrounded by difficult terrain, making them easy to defend but hard to take down. It is recommended that Governors make extensive preparations before launching an attack.
1. Keep Sentinels and Keep Patrols actively attack troops that enter the vicinity of the keep, pay attention to your positioning.
2. Attacking Keep Sentinels and Keep Patrols will not expend your action points.
3. Defeating Keep Sentinels and Keep Patrols will increase the Alertness level of Barbarian Keeps. Once Alertness is maxed out, their chieftain Bloodfist Bargha will spawn with his guards to defend the keep.
4. When you defeat Bloodfist Bargha, he will retreat back into the keep and throw up a protective shield. Once he regenerates, the Barbarian Keep becomes upgraded.
5. If you do not defeat Bloodfist Bargha within the time limit, he will retreat back into the keep and throw up a protective shield. When this protective shield ends, the Barbarian Keep becomes downgraded.
6. Higher level Barbarian Keeps contain stronger barbarians, and Bloodfist Bargha will drop even better rewards.

Keep Sentinel
Keep Sentinels are bloodthirsty warriors that will fight to the death to protect their chieftain.
1. Keep Sentinels actively attack troops that intrude their territory, pay attention to your positioning.
2. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking Keep Sentinels. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
3. Attacking Keep Sentinels will not expend your action points.
4. Defeating Keep Sentinels will increase the Alertness level of Barbarian Keeps. Once Alertness is maxed out, their chieftain Bloodfist Bargha will spawn with his guards.
5. Defeating Keep Sentinels will not grant any rewards, but defeating their chieftain -- "Bloodfist Bargha", will grant amazing rewards.

Bloodfist Bargha
Bargha is a powerful barbarian chieftain. His strength and cunning earned him the title "Bloodfist".
1. Bloodfist Bargha will actively attack troops that intrude his territory, please be careful.
2. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking Bargha. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
3. Attacking Bargha will not cost any action points.
4. All troops involved in the attack will receive a prize upon victory, so we suggest sending multiple troops or attacking together with your allies.
5. After defeating Bargha, governors that dealt damage to him will receive random rewards. Bargha has a chance to drop a chest that contains equipment blueprints, which will require gathering to collect.
Leaves In:
After the countdown, Bloodfist Bargha will retreat back into his Barbarian Keep. You will not be able to challenge him for some time.
Swindler Dhalruk
Dhalruk is an indigenous inhabitant of this secluded land. By way of his incredibly shady dealings, he managed to gain control over a vast underground trading network, and had accumulated an unimaginable amount of wealth and power that can even rival that of a nation.
1. Dhalruk will actively attack your troops to protect the treasure that he is carrying.
2. Defeat Dhalruk to receive a ton of rewards. He will always drop a chest that contains rare loot so try to take down Dhalruk as soon you see him.
3. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking Dhalruk. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
4. Attacking Dhalruk will not expend your action points.
Barbarian Arrow Tower)
Barbarian Arrow Towers are the primary structures of defense that protect Barbarian Keeps. It is positioned at strategic locations and guarded by heavy patrols, ready to shoot down anything that dares to move within their range.
1. Barbarian Arrow Towers will actively attack troops that enter their range of attack.
2. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking arrow towers. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
3. Attacking Barbarian Arrow Towers will not expend your action points.
4. Defeating arrow towers will increase the Alertness level of Barbarian Keeps. Once Alertness is maxed out, their chieftain Bloodfist Bargha will spawn with his guards.
5. Destroying arrow towers will not grant any rewards, but defeating Bloodfist Bargha will grant amazing rewards.7

Imperial Crane Palace#
An emperor once took control of new territory, and sent a team of soothsayers and court advisers to inspect it. They returned with exciting news: the land was unlike any they had ever seen before. Its natural feng shui was in perfect alignment. The emperor had a new palace built in a spot where it could be at the center of the land's chi flow. A few days after construction was complete, clouds and mists began to swirl around the palace walls, completely enveloping it in a miraculous haze. When the mists finally cleared, a flock of cranes were found to have made the palace their new home, to the emperor's great delight. He declared that the cranes were to be left undisturbed on the palace grounds, and hand-carved a plaque to be hung above his bedroom door, which reads, "Imperial Crane Palace".
Imperial Crane Palace (Permanent)
Adorns your city with special 2020 Anniversary event decorations. (Duration: Permanent)

Creepwood CabinY
Dark green marsh gas slowly bubbles up to the surface as the cool ground soothes my feet, weary from the long climb. The bright moonlight felt somehow bleak here, casting shadows of gloomy perfection on the dead branches, turning them into teeth and claws. The doors and window shutters have long since rotted out, with little remaining. The bare wooden strips left behind are like fangs, as though the cabin were baring its jagged teeth for any passers-by, promising them a journey from which they would never return. Rumor has it that a witch has been seen here, but who would dare to go in and see?

The majority of this exquisite and inspired architecture has been carved as one piece out of a mountain, causing many appreciative passers by to marvel at its ingenious design, wondering what brilliant mind conceived it. The foothills and stairs were completed much earlier, and already display the wear and tear of time. But the tower itself continues to shine as though it were first polished mere hours ago.

Harvest Cottage
The harvest season has arrived. Pile the crops on the ground however you like; they will take several days to count either way. First, let us take the best of the bunch to the kitchen for our Thanksgiving Dinner! As the smoke from our chimneys urges on the setting sun, it in turn calls those left in the field towards home. As the golden glow of the setting sun creeps shines its last rays through our windows, everything is gentle and beautiful.

A palace where royalty spend their summers, the exquisite and moving trees and plants provide comforting shade. The dazzling summer sun passes through the windows and shines on the smooth palace floor, causing the entire palace to shimmer as though under water. A cool breeze flows through the halls, cutting through the hot summer air. As the season draws to a close, the occupants are reluctant to leave the gentle, serene beauty of this place. They say summers are long here, but by the time they leave, it's nearly the end of autumn.

<b>Individual Credits</b>
1.Individual Credits can be earned from alliance technology donations, alliance help, alliance building construction, alliance gifts, and alliance reclaim. They can be used to purchase items at the Alliance Shop.
2. When you earn Individual Credits from contributing to alliance technology, alliance help, and alliance building construction, you will also earn an identical number of Alliance Credits for your alliance. Your alliance leader and officers can use Alliance Credits to restock items to the alliance inventory for you and other alliance members to purchase.
3. You can earn up to <color=#30718C>10,000</color> Individual Credits per day from alliance help, and up to <color=#30718C>20,000</color> from constructing alliance buildings. There is no daily limit to the amount of credits you can earn from alliance technology donations.
4. Individual Credits <color=#30718C>will not be lost</color> after leaving an alliance, and can be used after joining a new alliance.

Royal ManorS
The tall spire symbolizes the dignity of the royal family, while the round roof of this gorgeous palace brings it down to earth. Light and shadow dance between the window slats. The reliefs are exquisite in detail. It has not served as often as the royal family's home, but it has been maintained as though they are expected back tomorrow.

Golden Sled Cottager
When the first snows of winter came, the children began asking over and over when they could visit Santa Claus. The adults sighed, and quickly began decorating city hall. Cut down and decorate the most beautiful fir trees. Of course, we'll need plenty of lanterns and bells... and a sleigh! How could we forget the sleigh? Finally, the weather will handle covering it all with a nice coat of snow. Perfect! Now it's clearly Santa's home. The mailbox in city hall is full to bursting with letters to Santa. I have to wonder, will reading all these letters convince the governor to put on a red suit for some special "overtime"?

Frozen Fortress"
Legend has it that if you follow the North Star on a snowy night, you can find the Frozen Fortress. Ice and snow cover the tops of towers, almost as though the winter here has lasted for many years. It is hard to tell if this building's beautiful radiance comes from moonlight shining on the ice, or from the permanent aurora dancing across the night sky. But the story ends at its exquisite gate; no one who has entered has ever come back to describe what lies within. Is this magnificent palace of ice and snow a waking dream, or a frozen tomb?
Frozen Fortress (Permanent)J
Adorns your city with special Christmas decorations. (Duration: Permanent)?

<b><size=30>Legendary Tavern</size></b>
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b>
1. Governors can use Sovereign Keys or gems to open chests and get the items within. Every 10 times you open a chest, you are guaranteed one Legendary Commander or their sculptures. 
2. For every 200 chests you open during the event, you will be rewarded with a chance to choose your reward once. Please keep track of time left so you can be sure to get the reward of your choice!
3. During this event, if you acquire a commander (or their sculptures) whose Expertise you've already unlocked, it will be changed to Legendary Marks, which can be exchanged for items in the Exchange Shop.
4. Up to 2,000 chests can be opened per day. This limit resets daily at UTC 00:00.
5. At the end of the event, unused Legendary Marks and Sovereign Keys will be saved for use in the next instance of this event.

Lucky Lanterns
As a new year begins, spring washes over the land. The red plums blossom in the cold, bringing a breath of tranquility amidst the smoke of fireworks. Lotus flower lamps reflect each other, coating even the clear river water in a blanket of red light. Lanterns of every shape and size light up the night, warming many a hand and face. The Spring Festival is a feast of family reunion. It is the sound of firecrackers to drive out the old and welcome the new. It is the glow of candlelight on the eve of the new year. And it is a longing for those loved ones who have already enjoyed their final year. The sky lanterns, drifting upwards, send silent blessings and wishes to the heavens. I wonder if those on the other side will get the message?
Lucky Lanterns (Permanent)
Adorns your city with special Spring Festival decorations (Duration: Permanent). Grants a 2000 gem reward upon use if you have this theme already.

Receivable Resources Remaining
1. Resources protected by your storehouse cannot be transported. Only excess resources can be transported.
2. Governors can no longer receive resource transports from allies once they have reached their daily resource reception limit.
Boundless Lighthouse
The eternal flame of this lighthouse has guided travelers since time immemorial. Legends claim that before the lighthouse, many would become forever lost in the darkness. Taking pity on his subjects, the king had a lighthouse built which would stay lit at all times. The grateful people were loyal to their liege from that day forth, serving him faithfully and helping him build a vast empire. 
Boundless Lighthouse (Permanent)N
Adorns your city with special Osiris League decorations. (Duration: Permanent)
Divine Lighthouse
No one is sure which dynasty built this lighthouse. It has been here as long as anyone can remember. People ventured inside from time to time, but almost everyone gave up before reaching the top. "Those stairs are endless!" they always complained, yet the tower appears quite ordinary and not that tall from the outside. When two youths finally completed the climb, they came back mentally scarred. From that day forth, they muttered endlessly to themselves about seeing heaven itself.
Divine Lighthouse (Permanent)N
Adorns your city with special Osiris League decorations. (Duration: Permanent)

Next attack's action point cost4
Peacekeeping and Versatility commanders have the Insight talent, which reduces the action point cost of attacking barbarians. After defeating barbarians, if the troops move on to attack other barbarians rather than returning to their city, they will benefit from an additional reduction in action point cost.

Anti-Cheating Update[
Greetings Governor,
To ensure a fair gaming environment for all players, we will be upgrading our anti-cheating system to fight back against resource sellers, botters, and refund abuse. So far in 2020 we have already detected prohibited behavior by 1.13 million characters and issued corresponding punishments. We will continue to strictly punish all prohibited behavior, including:
1. Resource sellers. Heavy punishments will be issued to both buyers and sellers, including reclaiming purchased resources, deactivating certain game functions, and issuing permanent account bans.
2. Bots (including third-party programs such as 360 auto). Bots can completely imbalance the game for regular players and may even steal users' personal information. Players will be issued bans corresponding to the severity of their botting activity. Please remember to only download and update Rise of Kingdoms from official stores to ensure the safety of your account and characters.
3. Refund abuse. Refund abuse can severely impact fair gameplay and we will continue to take action against such behavior in the future. Governors will be issued punishment corresponding to the severity of their situation, ranging from reclaiming the refunded purchases or deactivating certain game functions to permanent account bans.
4. Third-party payments. For the safety of your account, we ask all governors to refrain from the use of any unofficial payment channels. These platforms are frequently linked to credit card theft, fraud, and unauthorized gift cards. Using them may result in the theft of your personal information, game account, or credit card information, and you run the risk of never receiving your purchase. After receiving your money, some platforms may then request a refund for your purchases, resulting in you losing resources or even receiving a permanent account ban for refund abuse. If you have any questions about in-app purchases, please contact us by tapping your avatar in the top left -> Settings -> Customer Support.
5. Account selling. We advise governors not to engage in the buying or selling of accounts. Such transactions are not authorized and may result in unusual account or even an account ban.
6. In-game discussion of any of the above prohibited behaviors is also prohibited. Accounts that frequently violate this rule will be muted.
We can't ensure a fair gaming environment for all players without your help and support. Please contact Customer Support if you notice any unusual activity by other players. Together we can eradicate cheating and other unfair behavior. 
Thank you for supporting Rise of Kingdoms!

At the same time, visitors are reminded that all empires inevitably come to an end. The crowd left quiet, but restless, thoughts of fame and fate echoing endlessly throughout these hallowed halls.

Island Fortress
Every night, as the tide comes in, the sounds of waves lapping at the rocks send the locals into a daze. Using the light of the tower, they search the deep, dark, roiling sea, watching for the moment where the chaos turns to calm, the moment when they will pray to the moon and the stars. A distant bell calls out to those offshore, a reminder that within the roar of the sea, the cry of the sea, and the music of the sea, lies the promise of a new day at this old fortress.

A gold engraved copper bull lies atop a golden edifice and stares into the endless distance while symbolically carrying the sacred flame atop its back. Current treasure reward
There are several ways to earn Prestige Points in the Lost Kingdom. Prestige Points cannot be earned after the Lost Kingdom ends.
<b>Earn Prestige Points by doing any of the following</b>
Severely wound/kill 10 Tier 1 units of another camp:                     <b>1</b> Prestige Point
Severely wound/kill a Tier 2 unit of another camp:                    <b>1</b> Prestige Point
Severely wound/kill a Tier 3 unit of another camp:                    <b>2</b> Prestige Points
Severely wound/kill a Tier 4 unit of another camp:                    <b>5</b> Prestige Points
Severely wound/kill a Tier 5 unit of another camp:                    <b>10</b> Prestige Points
<b>You can gain extra Prestige Points by doing any of the following when attacking/defending Colossi, Holy Sites, Sandswept Outposts, or Ancient Outposts</b>
Severely wound/kill 10 Tier 1 units of another camp:                     <b>1</b> Prestige Point
Severely wound/kill a Tier 2 unit of another camp:                    <b>2</b> Prestige Points
Severely wound/kill a Tier 3 unit of another camp:                    <b>4</b> Prestige Points
Severely wound/kill a Tier 4 unit of another camp:                    <b>15</b> Prestige Points
Severely wound/kill a Tier 5 unit of another camp:                    <b>30</b> Prestige Points

HEART'S DESIRE
Heart's Desire7
SELECT COMMANDERS TO RECEIVE SPECIALIZED REWARD CHESTS!
<b><size=30>Heart's Desire</size></b>
Ancient powers have converged to make wishes come true.
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b>
1. Choose <color=#EE7600>5 commanders</color> to receive specialized chests that contain plentiful custom rewards consisting of the chosen commanders and their sculptures.
2. Governors can use <color=#EE7600>Sovereign Keys</color> or <color=#EE7600>Gems</color> to open chests and get the items within. Every 10 times you open a chest, you are guaranteed one Legendary Commander or their sculptures.
3. <b><size=30>Legendary Tavern</size></b>
Only the greatest of heroes become legends.
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b>
1. Governors can use <color=#EE7600>Sovereign Keys</color> or <color=#EE7600>Gems</color> to open chests and get the items within. Every 10 times you open a chest, you are guaranteed one Legendary Commander or their sculptures. 
2. For every <b>200</b> chests you open during the event, you will be rewarded with a chance to choose your reward once. Please keep track of time left so you can be sure to get the reward of your choice!
3. During this event, if you acquire a commander (or their sculptures) whose Expertise you've already unlocked, it will be changed to <color=#EE7600>Legendary Marks</color>. <color=#EE7600>Legendary Marks</color> can be exchanged for items in the Exchange Shop during this event and the Heart's Desire event.
4. Up to <b>2,000</b> chests can be opened per day. This limit resets daily at UTC 00:00.
5. At the end of the event, unused <color=#EE7600>Legendary Marks</color> and <color=#EE7600>Sovereign Keys</color> will be saved for use in the next instance of this event. 
In the Legendary Tavern event, If you earn a legendary commander (or their sculptures) whose expertise you've already unlocked, they will be automatically exchanged for Legendary Marks. Unused Legendary Marks will be saved, and can be used in the next instance of this event.

Mighty Forest
Spring's rejuvenation is in the sprouting of trees, the singing of birds, the glinting of light on the water that flows where a frozen lake once lay; the forest itself dances back to life. Weeds are cleared from the flowerbeds, flower buds fill the air with sweetness. Melting snow drips from the eaves as the sun's warmth filters through a canopy of green. Colorful lanterns hang from branches as people unearth the mead that has been stored all winter, ready to toast to the arrival of the new season.
Mighty Forest (Permanent)
Adorns your city with special spring decorations (Duration: Permanent). If you own this theme already you will instead receive 2000 Gems.
Mighty Forest (7 Days)J
Adorns your city with special spring event decorations. (Duration: 7 days)
Call of Eternity
These mighty walls stand, indestructible, iron chains shimmering with cold light. Many-hued flags hang from the castle ramparts, and precious treasures lie within.
The bells resound, deep and sonorous; the drawbridge at the city gate descends. Knights in full armor prepare to suppress their vulgar enemy. Now is the prelude to war. Lions and eagles stand guard, watching stone-silent as they leave. When they return triumphant, the bell tower's joyful peals will rise to greet them.

Afraid of Infantry
damage taken from infantry:
<b>Severely Wounded</b>
Severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital. However, once the hospital is full, any further wounded units will die from lack of treatment.
<b>Slightly Wounded</b>
Slightly wounded units cannot fight, but once the battle ends, they will return to the city and automatically recover.
Coalition Marker
Coalition
Blockade Mode:
Peaceful
Aggressive
While marching, friendly troops can pass freely, but non-friendly troops will not be able to pass when blockaded.
When two opposing troops are blockading, different effects will occur depending on your Blockade Mode:
If you have chosen Peaceful Mode, your troops will stand their ground and only move once their March Route is clear. If you have chosen Aggressive Mode, your troops will launch an attack on the blockading enemy (unless they are a rallied army) and only move once the target is gone.
Note: Attacking a blockading target in Aggressive Mode will automatically activate War Frenzy and cancel your Peace Shield.
Rallied armies will Blockade in Peaceful Mode by default, regardless of which Blockade Mode you have chosen. When Blockading, rallied armies will not actively attack.
Tap to Startm
To switch to ranged mode, your troop must consist solely of siege units and be using specific Combat Tactics.

<b><color=#FF0000>IMPORTANT REMINDERS</color></b>
1. Ensure your device and internet connection are stable before deleting your account. You may contact Customer Support within 15 working days of deleting your account to cancel the process, so please take note of the time and proceed with caution when deleting your account.
2. Once the deletion process is complete, you will not be able to log in, use, or restore your account. All personal information stored within your account will be either deleted or anonymously stored, and cannot be retrieved.
3. Once the deletion process is complete, all in-game currency, items, and other paid services will also be removed. Please proceed with caution when deleting your account.

Booking And Starting
How To Play0
1. This event must be <b>booked or started</b> by an <b>alliance leader or titled officer</b>. During the event, there are no limits to the <b>number of times you can participate</b>, <b>number of contestants</b>, or <b>time you can spend participating</b>.
2. If you select <b>Start Now</b>, the event will begin after a 5 minute countdown. This countdown cannot be canceled. If a start time has already been booked, you must cancel the booking before you can select Start Now.
3. Select <b>Book Start Time</b> to choose a time to begin the event. After the booking has been made, the event will begin after a 5 minute countdown, which begins at the booked start time. Before the booking time, you may cancel at any time. Bookings can only be made for the same day (i.e. you cannot make a booking for the next day).
1. This event must be <b>booked or started</b> by an <b>alliance leader or titled officer</b>. During the event, there are no limits to the <b>number of times you can participate</b>, <b>number of contestants</b>, or <b>time you can spend participating</b>.
Start Now
After selecting <b>Start Now</b>, the event will begin after a 1 minute countdown. This cannot be canceled. If a start time has already been booked, you must cancel the booking before you can select Start Now.
Book Start Time'
Select <b>Book Start Time</b> to select a time to begin the event. After the booking has been made, the event will begin after a 5 minute countdown which begins at the booked start time. You may cancel at any time. Bookings can only be made for that same day, and cannot be made days in advance.
1. Alliance Quiz is a game mode where <b>multiple alliance members answer online questions at the same time</b>. The event must be organized by an alliance leader or officer before you can enter. The alliance's total score is made up of the scores of all participating alliance members.
2. While answering questions, you can change your answer at any time before the countdown ends, and use Flashes of Insight and Chat Phrases to make sure that your fellow alliance members get the most points possible so that you can win big prizes.
3. The first player to answer correctly will gain extra points. Wrong or unfilled answers gain 0 points. Contestants must reach a certain number of points to gain alliance prizes.
Alliance Quiz is a game mode where <b>multiple alliance members answer online questions at the same time</b>. The event must be organized by an alliance leader or officer before you can enter. The alliance's total score is made up of the scores of all participating alliance members.
How To Play
During the quiz, you can change your answer at any point before the countdown ends. All players' answers can be seen by other alliance members, so you can work together with your allies to get the highest score!
Scoring Rules
<b>Answer correctly to gain points</b>! Wrong or unfilled answers will gain <b>0 points</b>. Each round, the scores of every contestant are added together to get the alliance's total score. The <b>first contestant to answer correctly</b> each round will get extra points. Contestants much reach a certain number of points to gain alliance prizes, so help your allies answer as many questions correctly as possible!
Exciting Gameplay
1. Flash of Insight: Use a Flash of Insight to take away one wrong answer that your allies can choose from. You can use up to <b>2</b> Flashes of Insight for each question. The amount of Flashes of Insight that can be used is shared between <b>alliance members</b>.
2. Chat Phrases: Use Chat Phrases to chat and discuss possible answers with your allies. A simple conversation can be of huge help to your allies!

If your rally target is an enemy Alliance Fortress, Crusader Flag, Pass, or Holy Site bordering your own alliance's territory, your rally will be designated as an aggressive rally, and its blockade mode will default to aggressive mode. Aggressive rallies will automatically attack enemy troops that blockade them on their march route.

3. Once your War Camp has been established, your aggressive rally will enter the preparation phase. A march route will appear between your War Camp and the rally target.
4. If the alliance territory that borders your rally target belongs to a Holy Site or a Pass, your War Camp will be built at an Alliance Flag or territory building adjacent to the Holy Site/Pass.

Anti-Cheating Update
Greetings Governor,
In order to ensure a fair experience for all governors, we have launched an anti-cheating system designed to combat prohibited resource transactions, third-party program interference, refund abuse, and other behavior of this nature. Governors found to be complicit in these or similar actions will be penalized as follows:
1. Resources in refunded purchases will now be reclaimed. Governors will receive a notification via in-game mail.
2. Governors who spam messages or post advertisements will be banned or muted, depending on the severity of the offense.
3. We have discovered governors abusing the refund policy, selling or purchasing resources in violation of game policies, and engaging in other actions that severely impact fair gameplay. Governors will be penalized according to the severity of their situation, ranging from a deactivation of certain game functions to permanent account bans.
4. To ensure the safety of your game accounts, we advise governors not to engage in activities that involve buying or selling accounts. These transactions are not protected by official authority and in the event of unusual activity and/or bans, the official authority will not take any actions. We also strictly prohibit in-game discussions related to buying and selling of accounts.
We ask all governors to refrain from the use of any unofficial payment channels. Third-party payment options are frequently linked to credit card theft, credit card fraud, and unauthorized gift cards. They may result in the theft of your personal information, game account, or credit card information. If you have any questions about in-app purchases, please contact us via Customer Support.
We will continue to combat cheating and ensure that all governors are playing fairly. Please report any suspicious activity or accounts to Customer Support. Together we can prevent abuse and provide the best, fairest gaming experience for everyone!
Thank you for supporting Rise of Kingdoms!

<b><size=22>Switching to Ranged Mode</size></b>
You can order a troop in an open area to switch to ranged mode. If you are in a War Frenzy and do not have a Peace Shield activated, troops in ranged mode will automatically launch ranged attacks against enemies within a certain range. You can also launch ranged attacks manually. Ranged mode cannot be used outside of your alliance's territory, and ranged attacks can only slightly wound units.

Thank you for playing Rise of Kingdoms. We take the protection of your personal information and privacy very seriously and invite you to read our {p1} and {p2} before continuing. If you agree, tap "Accept & Continue" to start playing.

User Agreement
Privacy PolicyR
Please note that we will collect and use the following data and permissions:
1. Your account name, email address, gender, device ID, and system-generated user ID are used for account identification, registration, and login, and to display your nickname and your age within in-game community pages. They are also used for security purposes, and to optimize our service and marketing functions.
2. Your in-game transaction records are used for security purposes and to optimize our customer support and service. Messages that you send within the app are collected to allow you to communicate with other players.
3. Your uploaded images, videos, and voice messages are used to allow you to send pictures and audio, post on in-game community pages, and upload pictures and set your avatar on your personal page.
4. Your in-app event records are used to improve our service and provide corresponding game features.
5. Your in-game internet browsing history is used to analyze in-game community access.
6. Your app crash logs and diagnostic data are used to improve our service and understand crash issues.z
Thank you for playing Rise of Kingdoms. We take the protection of your personal information and privacy very seriously and invite you to read our {p1} and {p2} before continuing. As part of our service, we will request the following permissions. If you agree, tap "Accept & Continue". After agreeing, your permissions can be enabled or disabled at any time in the Settings page.
Peaceful Mode
Troops in peaceful mode will block non-friendly troops from crossing through them, but will not attack first. Rallied armies can only be set to peaceful mode.
Aggressive Mode
Troops in aggressive mode will block and actively attack marching non-friendly troops. When entering battle they will activate the <b>War Frenzy effect</b>, which will <b>disable your Peace Shield</b>.)

In this event, governors can only use the trial commanders provided by the system. The trial commanders have a fixed level, and their talents, equipment, and formation cannot be changed. You can view detailed info about trial commanders in the trial commander preview screen. In addition, no governor buffs will have an effect on this event./
This event uses the following trial commanders:#
There are 6 missions in this event.
There will be three objectives in each trial mission: 1) Eliminate all enemies, 2) All commanders survive, and 3) Protect all Storehouses. Each objective completed will earn you a star, and you will unlock additional rewards the more stars you earn.
The Storehouse's resupply effect can be activated by the Governor sending a Troop into it, restoring the Troop's strength. Resupply operations prevent troops from moving.
Enemies will attack the storehouse from many different directions. Choosing the right commanders on the map to deal with each type of enemy will be essential.H

<b><size=22>Switching to Ranged Mode</size></b>
You can order a troop in an unoccupied space to switch to ranged mode. In this event, ranged mode troops will automatically attack enemy troops within a certain range. You can also order them to attack manually.
<b><size=30>How To Play</size></b>
1. You can challenge other governors in the Sunset Canyon to earn rating points and rewards.
2. You will be given <color=#855151>{p1} free challenge attempts</color> per day. Additional attempts will cost <color=#855151>1 Challenge Ticket per attempt</color>. Challenge Tickets can be earned by completing Daily Objectives. <color=#855151>Unused Challenge Tickets will be lost at the end of the season</color>.
3. Following each challenge, the victorious governor will earn rating points and the defeated governor will lose rating points. You can claim daily rewards based on your rating points.
4. Each Sunset Canyon season will last {p2} days. At the end of the season, you can claim season rewards based on your final ranking. Points will be reset after each season.
5. When a new season begins, you must <color=#855151>complete 1 Sunset Canyon challenge or set a defensive lineup</color> to receive an initial rating and participate in the season rankings.
6. Only buffs from <color=#855151>commander levels, talents, equipment, Museum Relics, formations, armaments, and VIP Unit Capacity bonuses</color> will take effect in the Sunset Canyon.
<b><size=30>Combat</size></b>
1. You can deploy up to 5 troops. The <color=#855151>unit composition of each troop will be determined automatically</color>.
2. You can place your troops in one of 8 squares. Each troop will first attack the front-row target directly in front of them, followed by the back-row target directly in front of them. If there are no targets directly in front, the troop will attack the closest target.
3. Defeat all of your opponent's troops within the time limit to win. If neither side has been eliminated by the end of the time limit, the side with the most units remaining will be the winner.e
<b><size=30>How To Play</size></b>
1. The Lost Canyon will be unlocked at the start of the Lost Kingdom event. When the Lost Kingdom ends, so will the Lost Canyon.
2. You will be given <color=#855151>{p1} free challenge attempts</color> per day. You can purchase up to {p2} more attempts per day with Gems. Your challenge attempts will reset every day at 00:00 UTC.
3. You can only challenge governors that are higher ranked than you. If you defeat them, you will swap rankings with them.
4. Canyon Coins you earn from the Lost Canyon can be kept and used in later seasons.
5. Only buffs from <color=#855151>commander levels, talents, equipment, Museum Relics, formations, armaments, and VIP Unit Capacity bonuses</color> will take effect in the Lost Canyon.
<b><size=30>Combat</size></b>
1. You can deploy up to 5 troops. The <color=#855151>unit composition of each troop will be determined automatically</color>.
2. You can place your troops in one of 8 squares. Each troop will first attack the front-row target directly in front of them, followed by the back-row target directly in front of them. If there are no targets directly in front, the troop will attack the closest target.
3. Defeat all of your opponent's troops within the time limit to win. If neither side has been eliminated by the end of the time limit, the side with the most units remaining will be the winner.

Ranged PointsE
Killing/severely wounding different tiers of troops will earn you different amounts of points. Governors will be ranked by their total score.
Only kills after 2019/10/23 UTC will be counted.
Ranged attacks can only slightly wound units. Whenever you kill/severely wound an enemy troop's unit (e.g. using a melee troop) while you're also attacking it with a ranged troop, you will earn "ranged points". Ranged points are calculated separately from normal points. The more ranged damage you deal and the more enemy units killed/severely wounded, the more ranged points you'll earn.x
1. During this event, you can use <b>Compasses</b> to search for relics. At the end of each search, you will receive an armament.
2. Before searching you can <b>select your preferred formation</b>. Legendary armaments for this formation will be added to the reward pool as your <b>preferred rewards</b>. You can change your preferred formation during the event. Changing your preferred formation will also change the armaments set as your preferred rewards.
3. You are guaranteed to receive a legendary armament once every <b>10</b> searches. You are guaranteed to receive an inscribed legendary armament for your preferred formation once every <b>{p1}</b> searches .
4. At the end of the event, unused Compasses will be converted into 200 Gems per Compass.
5. You must reach city hall <b>level 8</b> to take part in this event.
6. You must first build the state forum to equip armaments.

<b><size=22>Switching to Ranged Mode</size></b>
You can order a troop in an unoccupied space to switch to ranged mode. If you are in a War Frenzy and do not have a Peace Shield activated, troops in ranged mode will automatically launch ranged attacks against enemies within a certain range. You can also launch ranged attacks manually. Ranged mode cannot be used outside of your alliance's territory. In Heroic Anthem - Power Up, ranged attacks can severely wound enemy units, but will trigger a counterattack from the target troop.

<b><size=22>Switching to Ranged Mode</size></b>
You can order a troop in an unoccupied space to switch to ranged mode. If you are in a War Frenzy and do not have a Peace Shield activated, troops in ranged mode will automatically launch ranged attacks against enemy troops within range that are currently attacking your troops. You can also launch ranged attacks manually. Ranged mode cannot be used outside of your alliance's territory.
Crowning Glory
The tyrant king's men removed the crown from the head of the defeated lord, bringing it back to their liege across the mountains. Inspired by his plundered crown, the king raised a magnificent citadel on the site of the battle. The king painted his citadel in brilliant and enduring hues of gold and blue, conveying to all who laid eyes upon it that his legacy would last as long as the sun and sky themselves.

<b>Event Rules</b>
1. During this event, you can earn additional rewards each day you purchase at least <b>{p1}</b> Gems.
2. The last day of rewards includes Commander Sculptures for a commander of your choice. The range of commanders you can choose depends on your current Season. You can change your chosen commander at any time before you claim the last day's rewards.
3. Only Gems earned directly through recharges and bundle purchases will count towards the total. Gems received from other sources, including the Growth Fund, daily login rewards from the Supply Depot, bonus Gem items from Super-Value Bundles, bonus Gems from the Gem Store, and bonus Gem items from First Recharge Gift Packages, will not be counted.
4. Your total Gems purchased will be reset daily at <b>00:00 UTC</b>. Gems exceeding the daily requirement will not be counted toward the next day's total, nor will the current total be carried on to the following day.
5. Please make sure to claim your rewards before the end of the event.
6. You cannot take part in this event until your City Hall reaches level <b>8</b>.

SMITHY SPECIALS
Smithy Specials
To thank you for your support and patronage, the old Blacksmith is holding a special promotion on equipment blueprints! He's opened up his special Blacksmith's Reserve just for you!1
<b><size=30>Smithy Specials</size></b> 
As a thank you for your continued patronage, the Blacksmith is running an exclusive promotion!
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b> 
1. You can select your <color=#EE7600>preferred equipment blueprint</color> from the Blacksmith's Reserve. During the Smithy Specials event, you will be able to purchase fragments of your preferred blueprint. While the event is ongoing, you can change your preferred equipment blueprint whenever.
2. During the event, you can purchase items with <color=#EE7600>Gems or Tempered Hammers</color>.
3. After purchasing an item, the happy old Blacksmith will pick a random item you haven't purchased yet and knock its price down even further! Please note that <color=#EE7600>each item can only have its price reduced this way once</color>!
4. Once you've purchased all items on offer, the store will refresh automatically. Alternatively, you can pay the Blacksmith a <color=#EE7600>Tempered Hammer</color> to refresh the store.
5. At the end of the event, unused Tempered Hammers will be converted into Gems at a ratio of <color=#EE7600>1:{p1}</color>.
6. To select engineering equipment from the Blacksmith's Reserve, your kingdom must be in the Season of Conquest.
7. Your City Hall must be at least <color=#EE7600>Level 8</color> to participate in this event.+

Nestled in the deepest woods, this storied temple cuts through the moonlight, a relic of some polis long-forgotten. Here, the epics of countless heroes were forged.
Once, the breezes here carried the melody of plucked lyre strings, the hyacinths grew blood-red, and minstrels sang of lost loves. Little by little, these memories and melodies have faded. It is now little more than the burial ground of forgotten dreams, but perhaps tomorrow the olive trees will bloom anew.

<b><size=30>Heart's Desire</size></b>
 <color=#997336>Ancient powers have converged to make wishes come true.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Open chests to earn rewards.
 <b>How to Play</b>
1. Choose <color=#379f1f>5 commanders</color> to create an exclusive chest.
2. You can change your <color=#379f1f>chosen commanders</color> at any time before the end of the event.
3. Open chests with <color=#379f1f>Sovereign Keys</color> or <color=#379f1f>Gems</color>.
4. If you earn a Legendary commander (or their sculptures) during this event and you have already unlocked that commander's expertise, they will be automatically exchanged for <color=#379f1f>Sovereign Keys</color>.
 <b>Rewards</b>
The exclusive chest contains bountiful rewards, including your chosen commanders (or their sculptures).
 <b>Guaranteed Rewards</b>
Every 10 chests you open guarantees a Legendary commander or their sculpture.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
1. You can open up to <color=#E06666>{p1}</color> chests per day. This limit resets every day at UTC 00:00.
2. At the end of the event, unused Sovereign Keys will not be lost, and can be used in the next instance of this event.

You can order a troop in an unoccupied space to switch to ranged mode. If you are in a War Frenzy, troops in ranged mode will automatically launch ranged attacks against enemy troops within range that are currently attacking your troops or allied troops. Additional Damage Bonus
Governor, your game seems to be crashing due to an issue with the old in-game chat system. Would you like to switch to the <b>pioneer version</b> of the chat?
Pioneer Chat System
If enabled, the pioneer version of the in-game chat will be enabled the next time you start the game. If disabled, the classic in-game chat will be enabled the next time you start the game.J
Barbarian Keeps are important strategic positions that are usually surrounded by difficult terrain, making them easy to defend but hard to take down. You'll need to make extensive preparations before launching an attack. 
1. Keep Sentinels and Keep Patrols will actively attack troops that enter the vicinity of their Keep. Remember to pay close attention to your positioning. 
2. Attacking Keep Sentinels and Keep Patrols does not cost action points. 
3. Defeating Keep Sentinels and Keep Patrols will increase the Alertness level of their Barbarian Keep. Once a Keep's Alertness is maxed out, the chieftain Bloodfist Bargha will spawn with his guards to defend the Keep. 
4. When you defeat Bloodfist Bargha, he will retreat back into the Keep and throw up a protective shield. Once he regenerates, the Barbarian Keep will be upgraded. 
5. If you do not defeat Bloodfist Bargha within the time limit, he will retreat back into the Keep and throw up a protective shield. When this protective shield ends, the Barbarian Keep will be downgraded. 
6. Higher-level Barbarian Keeps contain stronger barbarians, and Bloodfist Bargha will drop even better rewards.
7. Barbarian Keeps in the Lost Kingdom will also have Keep resource centers. After defeating Bloodfist Bargha, you can occupy the Keep's resource centers to gather resources there for some time.
1. Defeating Keep Sentinels or Arrow Towers will not earn you any rewards, but helping to defeat <b>Bloodfist Bargha</b> will earn you massive rewards.
2. When Bloodfist Bargha appears, there is a chance that <b>Swindler Dhalruk</b> will spawn too. When Bargha and Dhalruk are killed, they may drop chests containing equipment blueprint fragments.
3. In the <b>Lost Kingdom</b>, you can only attack Bloodfist Bargha if you are in an alliance. If you are not in an alliance, you will not increase the Keep's Alertness when defeating Keep Sentinels, Arrow Towers, and Patrols. The alliance of the governor who defeats Bargha will be able to occupy the Keep's resource centers and gather resources there for some time.

KEEP RESOURCE CENTERS
The alliance of the governor who defeats Bloodfist Bargha will be able to occupy the Keep's resource centers and gather resources there for some time. Keep resource centers are completely safe gathering points, as enemy troops cannot attack troops gathering resources inside them. 
1. All alliance members can gather resources from a Keep resource center, but each member can only dispatch 1 gathering troop per resource center. 
2. Keep resource centers will disappear 64 hours after they are occupied, regardless of whether all the resources have been gathered.
3. The higher the level of the Keep, the greater the output and resource reserves of its resource centers.Y
Your troops will never be attacked by enemies when gathering from a Keep resource center.
Baulur is a barbarian chieftain. His powerful physique and brutality have earned him the title "Ironhand".
1. Ironhand Baulur and his Camp Wanderers will actively attack any of your troops within his territory. As long as Ironhand Baulur remains alive, more Camp Wanderers will continuously spawn and join the battle.
2. Ironhand Baulur will disappear after some time, so try to defeat him as quickly as possible.
3. Your units will only be slightly wounded when fighting Ironhand Baulur. Slightly wounded units will recover once they return to your city.  
4. Attacking Ironhand Baulur does not cost any action points.  
5. All governors involved in the attack will receive a prize upon victory, so we suggest sending multiple troops or attacking together with your allies.
6. Ironhand Baulur has a chance to drop a chest containing equipment blueprints, which will require gathering to collect.
7. Each governor can attack Ironhand Baulur {p1} times per day. You have attacked him {p2} time(s) today.0
Keep Sentinels are bloodthirsty warriors that will fight to the death to protect their chieftain.
1. Keep Sentinels will actively attack troops that enter the vicinity of their Keep. Remember to pay close attention to your positioning.
2. Your units will only be slightly wounded when fighting Keep Sentinels. Slightly wounded units will recover once they return to your city.
3. Attacking Keep Sentinels does not cost action points.
4. Defeating Keep Sentinels will increase the Alertness level of their Barbarian Keep. Once a Keep's Alertness is maxed out, the chieftain Bloodfist Bargha will spawn with his guards.
5. Defeating Keep Sentinels will not earn you any rewards, but defeating Bloodfist Bargha will earn you massive rewards.
6. After defeating Bloodfist Bargha, the remaining Keep Sentinels will flee.
Arrow Towers are a Barbarian Keep's main defensive fortifications. They are positioned at strategic places and constantly manned, ready to shoot down anything that dares move within their range.
1. Barbarian Arrow Towers will actively attack troops that enter their attack range. Remember to pay close attention to your positioning.
2. Your units will only be slightly wounded when fighting Arrow Towers. Slightly wounded units will recover once they return to your city.
3. Attacking Barbarian Arrow Towers does not cost action points.
4. Destroying Arrow Towers will increase the Alertness level of their Barbarian Keep. Once a Keep's Alertness is maxed out, the chieftain Bloodfist Bargha will spawn with his guards.
5. Destroying Arrow Towers will not earn you any rewards, but defeating Bloodfist Bargha will earn you massive rewards.
6. After defeating Bargha, the Arrow Towers in the Keep will be destroyed.y
Keep Patrols guard their Keep day and night. Even more ferocious than Keep Sentinels, once these barbarians lock on to an enemy, they will pursue their prey to the ends of the earth.
1. Keep Patrols will actively attack troops that enter the vicinity of their Keep. Remember to pay close attention to your positioning.
2. Your units will only be slightly wounded when fighting Keep Patrols. Slightly wounded units will recover once they return to your city.
3. Attacking Keep Patrols does not cost action points.
4. Defeating Keep Patrols will increase the Alertness level of their Barbarian Keep. Once a Keep's Alertness is maxed out, the chieftain Bloodfist Bargha will spawn with his guards.
5. Defeating Keep Patrols will not earn you any rewards, but defeating Bloodfist Bargha will earn you massive rewards.
6. After defeating Bloodfist Bargha, the remaining Keep Patrols will flee.$

<b><size=30>SMITHY SPECIALS</size></b> 
As a thank you for your continued patronage, the Blacksmith is running an exclusive promotion!
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b> 
1. You can select your <color=#EE7600>preferred equipment blueprint</color> from the Blacksmith's Reserve. During the Smithy Specials event, you will be able to purchase fragments of your preferred blueprint. While the event is ongoing, you can change your preferred equipment blueprint whenever.
2. During the event, you can spend <color=#EE7600>Tempered Hammers or other items</color> to purchase the items for sale. Please visit the shop to see the specific cost of each item.
3. After purchasing an item, the happy old Blacksmith will pick a random item you haven't purchased yet and knock its price down even further! Please note that <color=#EE7600>each item can only have its price reduced this way once</color>!
4. Once you've purchased all items on offer, the store will automatically refresh. Alternatively, you can pay the Blacksmith <color=#EE7600>Gems or Tempered Hammers</color> to manually refresh the store. Starting with refresh no. {p2}, you will no longer be able to use Gems to refresh the store and will need to use Tempered Hammers.
5. At the end of the event, unused Tempered Hammers will be converted into Gems at a ratio of <color=#EE7600>1:{p1}</color>.
6. To select engineering equipment from the Blacksmith's Reserve, your kingdom must be in the Season of Conquest.
7. Your City Hall must be at least <color=#EE7600>Level 8</color> to participate in this event.

they say it's warmest right before the frost takes you. They would never again see the spring, and the emperor would never again smile.

1. Select a location on the map to open the interaction bubble, then tap the Attack March button.
2. When attack marching, your troops will <color=#379f1f>automatically attack</color> any <color=#379f1f>enemy troops</color> they encounter along the way.
3. If a target troop leaves your troop's pursuit range or disappears from the map (i.e. if they are defeated or enter a building), your troop will stop pursuing the target and continue on to its <color=#379f1f>original destination</color>.
4. Troops will not attack buildings or rallied armies while attack marching.
5. If you order multiple troops to attack march and they have multiple enemy troops within their pursuit range, your troops will not necessarily attack the same target.
6. Troops capable of changing to ranged mode can also attack march.

You Can Now Gift Flux ScripsU
Greetings Governor,
Congratulations! You've unlocked the ability to gift Flux Scrips! You'll now be able to gift any Flux Scrips you purchase to your fellow alliance members.
Please note that there is a limit to how many Flux Scrips you can send within a certain time frame. In addition, Flux Scrips cannot be manually withdrawn after they are sent. Also note that if you request a refund for Flux Scrips you've gifted to someone else, those Flux Scrips will be removed from the recipient's account. Please use this feature with caution!
Thank you for your support. Have fun and enjoy the game!

THE KING'S RETURN5

Wat Phra Si Sanphet4
Wat Phra Si Sanphet is a grand Buddhist temple ("wat") located in the old royal palace of the Ayutthaya Kingdom (in modern-day central Thailand). It served as the royal temple until the kingdom moved its capital, reserved for the exclusive use of the royal family. Monks were not allowed to live there and required an invitation to visit. Over the course of the kingdom's history, Wat Phra Si Sanphet hosted countless important religious and ceremonial events for the Ayutthaya royal family.
Today, only the temple's three grey-and-white stupas (known as "chedi" in Thailand) remain standing, and they have come to serve as a symbol for the Ayutthaya Kingdom as a whole. As embodiments of Thailand's unique cultural heritage, the three chedi are celebrated as a historical treasure both in Thailand and around the world.

PREFERRED FORMATIONK
1. Before starting the event, you must select your preferred formation. All armaments in the event shop will be for your preferred formation.

Event Rules
Icon Explanation~
<b><size=30>Armament, Reveal Thyself</size></b>
 <color=#997336>Reveal the secrets of the armaments and take them home!</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Use <color=#379f1f>Armament Tickets</color> to claim mysterious armaments and reveal their full potential.
 <b>Starting the Event</b>
1. Before Starting:
Select your <color=#379f1f>preferred formation</color>. All armaments in the event shop are guaranteed to be for your preferred formation, though further information about them may be partially hidden.
2. During the Event:
Use Armament Tickets to purchase the armaments in the event shop and reveal their full information. Different armaments will require a different number of Armament Tickets.
The shop will automatically refresh every day at 00:00 UTC. You can also manually refresh the shop up to <color=#379f1f>{p1}</color> times for free. Subsequent manual refreshes will cost Gems.
You can also change your preferred formation. You will need to <color=#379f1f>refresh the shop, either automatically or manually</color>, for the shop to update with armaments for your new preferred formation.
3. After the Event:
Unused Armament Tickets will not be lost, and can be used in the next instance of this event.
 <b>Bonus Rewards</b>
You can earn additional rewards by spending a certain number of Armament Tickets:
- Preferred Formation Legendary Armament I: A Legendary armament for your preferred formation. All three attribute bonuses will be for the same unit type. Attribute bonuses will NOT be for siege units.
- Preferred Formation Legendary Armament II: A Legendary armament for your preferred formation. Guaranteed to have an inscription.
 <b>Requirements</b>
1. Your City Hall must be at least <color=#379f1f>level 8</color> to participate in this event.
2. If you immigrate during this event, the event shop will immediately refresh, and you will need to re-select your preferred formation.

Troops March at Same Speed (Map)
If enabled, whenever you select multiple troops and order them to march to a location, the troops will all move at the same speed (ranged mode troops will march slightly behind the rest of the troops).
Killing/severely wounding different tiers of units will earn you different amounts of points. Your Kill Leaderboard ranking will be based on your total kill points.
Only kills after 2019/10/23 UTC will be counted.
If you are in a scenario battle where ranged attacks can only slightly wound units, whenever you kill/severely wound an enemy troop's unit (e.g. using a melee troop) while you're also attacking it with a ranged troop, you will earn "ranged points". Ranged points are calculated separately from normal points. The more ranged damage you deal and the more enemy units killed/severely wounded, the more ranged points you'll earn.
In game modes like Ark of Osiris, the kill points you earn will be limited to that game mode and will not increase your actual kill point score.

<b><size=30>How To Play</size></b>
1. The Lost Canyon will be unlocked at the start of the Lost Kingdom event. When the Lost Kingdom ends, so will the Lost Canyon.
2. You will be given <color=#855151>{p1} free challenge attempts</color> per day. You can purchase up to {p2} more attempts per day with Gems. Your challenge attempts will reset every day at 00:00 UTC.
3. You can only challenge governors that are higher ranked than you. If you defeat them, you will swap rankings with them.
4. Canyon Coins you earn from the Lost Canyon can be kept and used in later seasons.
5. Only buffs from <color=#855151>commander levels, talents, equipment, Museum Relics, formations, armaments, and VIP Unit Capacity bonuses</color> will take effect in the Lost Canyon.
<b><size=30>Combat</size></b>
1. You can deploy up to <color=#855151>6 troops</color>. The <color=#855151>unit composition of each troop will be determined automatically</color>.
2. You can place your troops in one of 8 squares. Each troop will first attack the front-row target directly in front of them, followed by the back-row target directly in front of them. If there are no targets directly in front, the troop will attack the closest target.
3. Defeat all of your opponent's troops within the time limit to win. If neither side has been eliminated by the end of the time limit, the side with the most units remaining will be the winner.s
If enabled, your troops'/city's icons and info about allied troops can be quickly hidden when fighting map battles.

AUTARCH POINT LEADERBOARD RULES
1. The Autarch Point Leaderboards will start tracking points after Level 4 Passes become semi-protected and will stop after the Great Ziggurat opens. There are three leaderboards: Kill Points, Death Points, and Heal Points.
2. After the list of Autarchs is finalized, governors who were not chosen as Autarchs but ranked in the top 10 of any of the three leaderboards will automatically receive 1 Autarch Testimony.
1st to 10th
Perform the following actions in the Lost Kingdom to earn Kill Points. You will not earn any more points after the Great Ziggurat opens.
Severely wound/kill 1 Tier 4 unit belonging to an enemy camp: {p1} point
Severely wound/kill 1 Tier 5 unit belonging to an enemy camp: {p2} points
Perform the following actions in the Lost Kingdom to earn Heal Points. You will not earn any more points after the Great Ziggurat opens.
Heal 1 Tier 4 unit: {p1} point
Heal 1 Tier 5 unit: {p2} points
Perform the following actions in the Lost Kingdom to earn Death Points. You will not earn any more points after the Great Ziggurat opens.
Lose 1 Tier 4 unit in battle: {p1} point
Lose 1 Tier 5 unit in battle: {p2} points,
Tier 5 units will be automatically assigned.

<b><size=30>In Search of Wonders</size></b>  
Countless relics of countless civilizations have been lost to the ravages of time.
Use mystical Compasses to seek out these lost treasures and add them to your ever-growing arsenal!
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b> 
1. During this event, you can use <color=#EE7600>Compasses</color> to search for armaments. After each search, you will receive an armament.  
2. After <color=#EE7600>selecting your preferred formation</color>, armaments for said formation will be added to the prize pool as your <color=#EE7600>preferred rewards</color>.
Legendary Armaments: You can select 1 preferred formation.
Epic Armaments: You can select 5 preferred formations.
3. Whenever you search, you have a chance of acquiring one of your preferred rewards. You can change your preferred formation during the event, which will change the armaments set as your preferred rewards. 
4. You are guaranteed to receive a legendary armament once every <b>10</b> searches. You are guaranteed to receive an inscribed Legendary armament for your preferred formation once every <b>{p1}</b> searches.
5. At the end of the event, unused Compasses will be converted into 200 Gems per Compass.
6. You must reach City Hall <color=#EE7600>level 8</color> to take part in this event.
7. You must first build the State Forum to equip armaments.A

Song of the Depths
Deep beneath the waves, water folds and unfolds like cr
pe paper.
Sunlight ripples through the depths, shimmering and scintillating, weaving threads of beauty into a tapestry of wonders. Swarms of jellyfish amble through, light exploding into color as it refracts through their translucent bodies.
The Witch of the Waves awakes among this reverie, her eyes still heavy with sleep. After all, it had taken half of yesterday and all of her patience to dissuade that love-mad little mermaid. She recalls the potion of forgetfulness she gave the girl, disguised as a salve that would let her walk among the surface folk. The sirens and serpents call the witch "Defender of the Deep". She thinks the nickname's well-deserved.
"I really mustn't sink any more ships," she thinks. After all, one never knows which of them might be carrying a handsome young prince, ready to disturb the peace of her sea.

Servers in Rise of Kingdoms are called "kingdoms".
Kingdom titles, such as Queen, General, etc. Titles can be assigned by the king or by governors with a "ruler" title (Queen, General, or Prime Minister). Each title comes with its own buffs or debuffs.
R4 members of an alliance, appointed by the alliance leader or Counselor from among the alliance's members. Alliance officers can also be assigned titles with unique buffs, such as Counselor or Warlord.
A type of building that alliances can build on the map, used to claim and control territory. Sometimes shortened to just "Flag".
When used as a verb, refers to the act of stationing troops in a stronghold. When used as a noun, refers to the troops stationed in a given stronghold.
A mechanic that allows you to gather troops from multiple governors to create a larger troop called a "rallied army". Rallied armies are primarily used to attack powerful targets, such as Barbarian Forts, cities, and strongholds. Rallied armies are often just referred to as "rallies".>
A group of multiple kingdoms with consecutive kingdom numbers.
