<b><size=30>ARMAMENT, REVEAL THYSELF</size></b>
<color=#997336>Reveal the secrets of the armaments and take them home!</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Use <color=#379f1f>Armament Tickets</color> to claim mysterious armaments and reveal their full potential.
 <b>Starting the Event</b>
1. Before Starting:
Select your <color=#379f1f>preferred formation</color>. All armaments in the event shop are guaranteed to be for your preferred formation, though further information about them may be partially hidden.
2. During the Event:
Use Armament Tickets to purchase the armaments in the event shop and reveal their full information. Different armaments will require a different number of Armament Tickets.
*The shop will automatically refresh every day at 00:00 UTC. If you have purchased all armaments on offer, the shop will refresh automatically. You can also manually refresh the shop up to <color=#EE7600>{p1}</color> times for free. Subsequent manual refreshes will cost Gems.
You can also change your preferred formation. You will need to <color=#EE7600>refresh the shop</color>, either automatically or manually, for the shop to update with armaments for your new preferred formation.
3. After the Event:
Unused Armament Tickets will not be lost, and can be used in the next instance of this event.
 <b>Bonus Rewards</b>
You can earn additional rewards by spending a certain number of Armament Tickets:
Preferred Formation Legendary Armament I: A Legendary armament for your preferred formation. All three attribute bonuses will be for the same unit type (if you've selected V formation as your preferred formation, there's a chance that the attribute bonuses will be for siege units).
Preferred Formation Legendary Armament II: A Legendary armament for your preferred formation. Guaranteed to have an inscription.
 <b>Requirements</b>
1. Your City Hall must be at least <b>level 8</b> to participate in this event.
2. If you immigrate during this event, the event shop will immediately refresh, and you will need to re-select your preferred formation.
If you do this, your total resources will exceed your Storehouse's protection capacity. Any excess resources you possess could be plundered by attacking enemies. Proceed?
Twilight Banquet
It was late October when I escaped from the front lines. The frost had begun early that year, and as I hid in that cold, dark forest, I could feel my fingers growing numb. But you must understand that nothing is worse than hunger
I hadn't eaten for three days. "Lord above!" I thought to myself, "Let me die freezing or exhausted, just not hungry!"
As I finished that thought, the trees before me parted to reveal an open clearing, lit up by the full moon. At the center of the clearing stood a tall and beautiful tower of quarried marble. An orange-yellow glow shined through its windows. I could smell the waft of delicious food and hear the sounds of laughter coming from inside.
I could only muster the strength to knock on the door before I collapsed from hunger. When I came to, it was already daybreak. The taste of roast chicken and earthy wine lingered on my tongue. My body felt warm and my stomach felt full. I got up, looking for someone to thank for the feast I had seemingly forgotten.
The tower had disappeared without a trace.

Having subdued the usurper Torgny and his rebel chieftains, we believed that we had brought peace to Ceroli... blind to the greater danger lurking in the wings. Story
Having subdued the usurper Torgny and his rebel chieftains, we believed that we had brought peace to Ceroli... blind to the greater danger lurking in the wings. A thick, unnatural miasma has descended upon the island, scattering our forces across the island. Under constant attack by the usurper's remaining loyalists, our isolated troops have been fighting just to survive, but there's still hope yet. Having linked up with a group of reliable allies, we might just be able to go on the offensive...
Introduction
Team up with your fellow governors to take on unknown challenges, earn powerful skills, and defeat mighty enemies! Clear the Realm of Mystique to earn untold riches and rewards!
Rules
<b><size=30>Realm of Mystique</size></b>
The island of Ceroli has been engulfed in a mysterious miasma. Can you make it through this unpredictable battlefield?
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b>
1. Participation Requirements
Your City Hall must be at least level <color=#EE7600>10</color> to take part in this event. Each difficulty mode also has its own power requirement you must meet to select it.
2. Difficulty
- This event is divided into Normal Mode and Legend Mode. 
In Normal Mode, your event rewards will be based on your power level. 
Starting from Season 2, you can unlock Legend Mode if you meet the power requirement. In Legend Mode, enemy Ceroli troops will be more powerful, and will gain two random Legend Mode skills. Completing the event in Legend Mode will earn you better rewards. 
3. Invites
You can tap the Invite button to share a challenge invitation link to other governors in any chat channel, and likewise join another governor's challenge through their invitation link.
*Governors who have not yet unlocked your selected difficulty mode will not be able to join your challenge through your link.
4. Matchmaking
- Governors in Normal and Legend Mode will be matched separately. In Normal Mode, you can enable "cross-power matchmaking" mode to match with governors of different power levels, which will allow you to find a match quicker and earn you extra buffs/rewards. If cross-power matchmaking is disabled, you will only be matched with teammates of the same power level as you. If your team has a member whose power level is different from yours, you will need to enable cross-power matchmaking.
- Matchmaking in this event is also cross-kingdom. Once a match has been made, tap the Ready button to confirm your participation. After all participants are ready, you will be automatically transported to the battlefield.
5. Challenge Rules
- This event is considered a scenario battle. The basic rules of combat are essentially the same as normal map battles, but the effects of potions and other special event mechanics will <color=#EE7600>only</color> take effect in this scenario battle.
- The Realm of Mystique consists of several stages, each containing unknown challenges and dangers.
- <color=#EE7600>3</color> of the stages (including the final stage) will feature an enemy chieftain. These chieftains are powerful foes, but will drop valuable rewards when defeated. In the final stage, you and your team will have to face the mightiest Ceroli chieftain. Once you defeat them, you will have cleared the Realm of Mystique.
- Your progress will be <color=#EE7600>saved</color> whenever you defeat a chieftain. If all of your team's troops are defeated, you will be returned to your last save. If you choose to quit, your progress will be fully reset.
- Once you've cleared a stage's objectives, a <color=#EE7600>teleport camp</color> will appear on the battlefield. Once at least half of all governor troops have entered the camp, your team will be transported to the next stage.
- Each governor can only dispatch one troop to the battlefield.
6. Units
In this event, the units you can deploy are provided by the system. Any casualties you suffer will not affect your units outside of this game mode.
7. Rewards and Rewards Chances
- On Ceroli chieftain stages, you will earn a reward for defeating the chieftain. On Normal Mode, you will be rewarded based on your power level. On Legend Mode, the rewards will be increased.
- For the duration of each instance of this event, you can challenge the Realm of Mystique an unlimited number of times, but you can <color=#EE7600>only earn a reward from each chieftain once</color>. If it is not your first challenge, you cannot claim extra rewards from cross-power matchmaking or Legend Mode. This limit will reset the next time the event begins.
- If you quit midway or contribute less than 1% of your team's total contribution points, you will not earn a reward.
8. Balanced Mode
Balanced Mode has been enabled for this event. Technology and civilization bonuses will be equalized for all participants, and all commanders will be provided with equipment and armaments of equal strength based on their unit specialties.
Commander levels, talents, and skill levels <color=#EE7600>will take effect</color> in this event.
Buffs from city themes, Runes, alliance technologies, and certain items (e.g. Attack Enhancement, Defense Enhancement, and Army Expansion) <color=#EE7600>will not take effect</color> in this event.
9. Ceroli Coins
Unused Ceroli Coins can be <color=#EE7600>kept</color> and used in the next instance of this event.

<b>Individual Credits</b>
1. Individual Credits can be earned from alliance technology donations, alliance help, alliance building construction, alliance gifts, and alliance recycling. They can be used to purchase items at the Alliance Shop.
2. When you earn Individual Credits from contributing to alliance technology, alliance help, and alliance building construction, you will also earn an equal number of Alliance Credits for your alliance. Your alliance leader and officers can use Alliance Credits to restock the alliance inventory with items for you and other alliance members to purchase.
3. You can earn up to <color=#379f1f>10,000</color> Individual Credits per day from alliance help, and up to <color=#379f1f>20,000</color> from constructing alliance buildings. There is no daily limit to the amount of Credits you can earn from alliance technology donations.
4. Individual Credits <color=#379f1f>will not be lost</color> after leaving an alliance, and can be used after joining a new alliance.
5. If your alliance has a member limit of 100 or more, you can spend your Individual Credits to purchase items from the "Weekly Limit" section. The items in this section will be automatically restocked once per week without costing your alliance any Alliance Credits.
Note: Resources and speedups from Resource Packs and Pick One chests are not shown here.w

1. Through the transmog feature, you can change the <b>appearance</b> of a city theme to that of another city theme while <b>keeping its original attribute bonuses</b>.
2. Transmogging requires Little Brushes:
The first time you transmog City Theme A into City Theme B, you will need to spend Little Brushes.
Any subsequent times you transmog City Theme A into City Theme B, you will not need to spend Little Brushes.
3. After transmogging a theme, you can restore it to its original appearance at any time via the transmog interface. Restoring a transmogged theme does not cost any items.4

GATHERING OF HEROES
Gathering of HeroesH
<b><size=30>Gathering of Heroes</size></b>
<color=#997336>Get the latest and mightiest Legendary commanders.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Complete event quests to earn points, which can be used to recruit Legendary commanders that you don't own.
 <b>How to Play</b>
1. <color=#379f1f>Complete quests</color> to earn event points.
2. Daily quests and rewards can be found on the event page.
 <b>Rewards</b>
Use event points to recruit powerful Legendary commanders that you don't own.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
1. Daily quests will be <color=#E06666>reset</color> every day at 00:00 UTC, so remember to claim your rewards.
2. Spend the required amount of points to unlock the next tier.
3. The amount of points needed to recruit commanders will increase as your tier increases. All commanders in the same tier cost the same amount of points to recruit.
4. You can only recruit commanders that <color=#E06666>you don't already own</color>.
5. If you immigrate during this event, you will no longer be able to participate, regardless of if you participated in your original kingdom or not.k

<color=#ea8604>Higher than <b>{p1}%</b> governors</color>5
Higher than <b>{p1}%</b> of governors in your kingdom:
Higher than <b>{p1}%</b> of governors in this Lost Kingdom
1. Your total power is the sum of your building, technology, troop, commander, and equipment power. You'll receive a letter rating for each category based on your power. 
2. Certain categories have special rules for calculating your rating:
- Your commander rating will not factor in commanders who aren't usable in your current season.
- Your equipment rating will not factor in equipment that isn't usable in your current season. Your rating will only factor in your 7 most powerful pieces of equipment in each category, with the exception of accessories. For accessories, your rating will include your 14 most powerful pieces.
- Because of these rules, your ratings for these categories may not be 1:1 with your power.
3. If your city is in your original kingdom, your power will be compared against other governors in your kingdom. In the Lost Kingdom, your power will be compared against other governors in your current Lost Kingdom.
1. Your total power is the sum of your building, technology, troop, commander, equipment, and armament power. You'll receive a letter rating for each category based on your power. 
2. Certain categories have special rules for calculating your rating:
- Your commander rating will not factor in commanders who aren't usable in your current season.
- Your equipment rating will not factor in equipment that isn't usable in your current season. Your rating will only factor in your 7 most powerful pieces of equipment in each category, with the exception of accessories. For accessories, your rating will include your 14 most powerful pieces.
- Your armament rating will only factor in your 7 most powerful armaments in each category (e.g. Scrolls, Instruments, etc). Your rating will also factor in these armaments' inscriptions and attributes.
- Because of these rules, your ratings for these categories may not be 1:1 with your power.
3. If your city is in your original kingdom, your power will be compared against other governors in your kingdom. In the Lost Kingdom, your power will be compared against other governors in your current Lost Kingdom.

Fearlessly Forth
Glazed tiles and delicate golden filigree sit atop a beast of gears and smoke.
The bull parts clouds, shakes mountains, and moves the immovable. The people look up in awe at this strange marriage of timeless elegance and unbridled modernity, as if had been dropped down from the Nine Heavens by some bored and creative demigod.
In truth, it is the work of mortal hands. Thousands of them, working to build an unassailable edifice, a place of peace upon a creature of utmost violence.

Phoenix Perch
Wind parts cascading clouds for the stars, shining gilt-light upon the spire, in the endless space between reality and fiction.
I find myself above a sea of fey mountains, far past stirring mists and frothing springs. A phoenix's cry sends a thousand birds aflight, revealing the qilin's dance. I explore this ethereal scene for an endless dusk, unaware of passing eons. In this celestial realm, I am without desire, I am without sense, I am without importance. I am a droplet in an ocean, a grain of sand in a desert, a moment in a millennium.
When Zhuang Zhou awoke from his butterfly dream, did he truly not know if he had woken up? Or did he simply wish he was still dreaming?

Hangu Pass
Countless souls have perished beneath the stout walls of Hangu Pass, where one man can hold against a thousand.
The gatehouse soars high into the clouds, its eaves turned up to the heavens. It is a simple and solemn place, a warrior who has held his post for untold years. When war comes to this place
and it surely comes
the wall bears a panoply of banners and shakes 'neath the thunder of drums.
It would take ten thousand men ten thousand years to take this place, and none would survive the attempt.

Poseidon's Sanctuaryh
Hail, Poseidon, you who governs all storms betwixt shores! The golden sun shines upon your sea, your chariot gliding across its shimmering surface. Your hippocampi pull you effortlessly past a bulwark of roiling waves, to a sanctuary known to you alone.
You are the ocean's wrath, who once struck the earth with his trident and brought forth the torrents of extinction. But so too are you the ocean's mercy, for it was you who drew back the flood. It was you who left Deucalion and Pyrrha new seas to sail and new islands upon which to prosper.
So long as the tide rises and falls, no ship may sail without your say.

Eternal Emperoro
will deal extra direct damage to 5 nearby enemy troops whenever it deals direct damage with Iron Pierces Bronze
1. Your kingdom's king and rulers (Queen, General, and Prime Minister) can grant titles to any governor whose city is in the kingdom.
2. The king can grant all titles. Rulers can grant Defender and Sinner titles.
3. Each governor can only hold one kingdom title.
4. Kingdom titles will be removed if the owner immigrates.
5. In Lost Kingdom domains, each domain has separate kingdom titles. Titles granted by a domain will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom. At the start of the matchmaking phase, the system will record which domain your king belongs to. Titles granted by this domain will take effect in both the Lost Kingdom and your original kingdom.
6. Kings, Queens, Generals, and Prime Ministers can use king skills.

If the units remaining of one of your dispatched troops falls below this threshold, they will stop auto peacekeeping and return to your city.J
1. You can assign idle commanders in your city to lead auto peacekeeping troops, which will seek out and automatically attack Barbarian Troops.
2. Manually giving an order to an auto peacekeeping troop will cause it to exit auto peacekeeping mode.
3. If you exit the game, your troops will immediately exit auto peacekeeping mode.
You can plan up to 100 Barbarian Troop attacks per auto peacekeeping session. If you enable the "Use AP Recovery Items" option, you will automatically use action point recovery items to replenish AP spent attacking Barbarian Troops.q
The action point cost shown here is just a projection. The actual AP cost may be affected by a number of factors.

Summit of the Supreme
From the palace of the gods, radiance trickles to earth like a rain of gemstones.
Jade light dances up and down the stone steps, lighting a path to an ancient altar. Wind slinks through the cracks in the limestone, as if whispering secrets from ages long past.
The early morning here is always solemn and tranquil, as if naught else exists in the world. Pray to the Sun, or sit down for a hot chocolate with Time. As one of the glorious few allowed within these halls, you may dance with the gods for as long as you wish.!

Sands of Timel
Time stood still within a prison of glass and gold.
The ruins of stout city walls, the decaying palace and its wind-worn statues
mere remnants of a dynasty that time forgot. Year after year, the yellow sands blew past, depositing layer after layer upon this history until it was well and truly buried.
It was not until the divine bird passed through here that, with a single beat of its wings, time began to flow again. The hourglass was upturned by the hand of fate, starting the count anew. The tiled dome regained its luster, golden filigree snaked through the stone once more, and the long-dry springs bubbled with new water. The royal palace has shaken off the dust of decay, as tall and proud as it was a thousand years prior.
Have you heard the prophecy, hidden beneath all this sand? That history will be written by your hand, the author of an eternal golden age.

1. Select a location on the map to open the interaction bubble, then tap the Attack March button.
2. When attack marching, your troops will <color=#379f1f>automatically attack</color> any <color=#379f1f>enemy troops</color> they encounter along the way.
3. If a target troop leaves your troop's pursuit range or disappears from the map (i.e. if they are defeated or enter a building), your troop will stop pursuing the target and continue on to its <color=#379f1f>original destination</color>.
4. Troops will not attack buildings or rallied armies while attack marching.
5. In the Combat Settings page, you can choose whether or not your attack marching troops will attempt to focus fire on the same enemy troop.
6. Troops capable of changing to ranged mode can also attack march.
7. Attack marching troops will automatically return to your city if their units remaining drops below a certain threshold.
8. If an attack marching troop is targeted by multiple enemy troops, it will automatically retreat backwards. Once it is no longer being attacked by multiple troops, it will resume seeking out enemies.
9. Attack marching troops will prioritize attacking enemy troops with low units remaining and as enemy troops with a primary unit type countered by their own.6
Includes additional resources for World Wanderer: Troy

Select
1. You can assign idle commanders in your city to lead auto peacekeeping troops, which will seek out and automatically attack Barbarian Troops.
2. Manually giving an order to an auto peacekeeping troop will cause it to exit auto peacekeeping mode.
3. If you exit the game, your troops will immediately exit auto peacekeeping mode.
4. Your auto peacekeeping troops will attempt to focus fire on Barbarian Troops when possible.

Through the transmog feature, you can <b>change the appearance</b> of a city theme to that of another city theme while <b>keeping its original attribute bonuses</b>.
Non-Kingdom Select city themes:
1. Transmogging requires Little Brushes.
The first time you transmog City Theme A into City Theme B, you will need to spend Little Brushes.
Any subsequent times you transmog City Theme A into City Theme B, you will not need to spend Little Brushes.
2. After transmogging a theme, you can restore it to its original appearance at any time via the transmog interface. Restoring a transmogged theme does not cost any items.
Kingdom Select city themes:
1. Transmogging between Kingdom Select city themes and non-Kingdom Select city themes is free.
2. Kingdom Select city themes <b>cannot be transmogged into other Kingdom Select city themes</b>.
3. If a Kingdom Select city theme becomes invalid, any transmogged city themes associated with it will have their effects reverted.*

Civilization Feature Reset
You must meet the following requirements to reset Civilization Features:
1. None of your troops (scouts excluded) are outside of the city or participating in a rally attack in your own city.
2. Neither your city nor your troops are in battle.
3. You have no troops training in your city.  
4. You have no troops healing in your city.
After resetting Civilization Features, your commanders will be unaffected, but your special units and feature bonuses will revert to that of their corresponding civilization.
Siege units gain <color=#ffc600><b>{p1}</b></color> Attack and deal <color=#ffc600><b>{p2}</b></color> more normal damage. Troop training speed is increased by <color=#ffc600><b>{p3}</b></color>.
Siege units gain <color=#ffc600><b>{p1}</b></color> Defense, hospital healing speed is increased by <color=#ffc600><b>{p2}</b></color>, and troop load is increased by <color=#ffc600><b>{p3}</b></color>.
When battling players of other Camps in Lost Kingdom:
 - Severely wounding 10,000 Lvl 1/2/3/4/5 units will grant {p1} Acclaim.
 - Killing 10,000 Lvl 1/2/3/4/5 units will grant {p2} Acclaim.
 - When 10,000 of your Lvl 1/2/3/4/5 units are severely wounded, you gain {p3} Acclaim.
 - When 10,000 of your Lvl 1/2/3/4/5 units are killed, you gain {p4} Acclaim.
Acclaim will be calculated starting from Lost Kingdoms opened after November 19, 2025.9

Phoenix Pavilione
The mountains rise and fall, vanishing and reappearing among the clouds. A gentle breeze stirs beneath my robes, carrying whispers from the celestial palaces veiled in mist.
The magnificent palaces glow with the crimson of sunset, once praised as a fairyland, now drifting lightly upon the wind, their paths shrouded in ethereal haze. Riding phoenixes and soaring beyond dragons, I glance back at the splendid pavilions hidden in the smoke, the soft strains of flutes drifting upon the breeze.

Flowing light and shadow mingle with cascading springs, sunlight spilling its brilliance, the vermilion palaces shimmering in layered radiance, while phoenixes exhale misty clouds into the sky. In times past, I sought the path to immortality; now, amidst these mountains, I feel as one among the immortals. Such a scene is rare in a lifetime. All former glories and worldly dust have faded. I soar upon a phoenix to the immortal banquet, leaning against the heavenly gate as I raise my voice in the song of eternity.

Field troops will replenish their units after a <b><color=#11fe00>10-second</color></b> countdown. If they take damage, the countdown will be interrupted. Civilization Feature Change
You must meet the following requirements to change Civilization Features:
1. None of your troops (scouts excluded) are outside of the city or participating in a rally attack in your own city.
2. Neither your city nor your troops are in battle.
3. You have no troops training in your city.  
4. You have no troops healing in your city.
After changing Civilization Features, your commanders will be unaffected, but your Civilization will switch to the bonuses of your selected feature.

Omniscient Watchv
O Horus, you who manifest the brilliance of stars and the light of flames, even the wind must bow to your Law.
The gods above await with bated breath and the mortals below lower their heads before your word, only because you are the hand that guides fate, the sigil that marks authority, and the end to all order and chaos.
Your every bout of thunderous flight sunders the sky, and every sweep of your burning gaze pierces through the darkness.
To witness your tales, one need only follow the Nile's flow, or look to the movement of celestial bodies.
The immortal and eternal Horus, he who reigns high, mover of the sun and stars.

100 Recovery Potions7
<b>Recovery Potions</b>
A potion traded through hands to honor brave governors. Use it to treat severely wounded units to help them return to the battlefield.
To heal each Tier 1/Tier 2/Tier 3/Tier 4/Tier 5 severely wounded unit requires 10/20/40/50/100 Recovery Potions, respectively.
When redeemed, Recovery Potions will not appear in the inventory and instead will be directly available for use in the Hospital. Recovery Potions will not expire until the end of the Season, at which point any unused potions will be converted into corresponding quantities of Food.
Recovery Potions can be used to immediately <b>treat severely wounded units in Hospitals</b> and do not expire until the end of the season.
All governors in the kingdom gain 30% Gathering Speed for 8 hours. While this skill is active, 1% of all resources gathered will be given to the king as taxes (up to 12 million per resource type). This skill can only be used by the king. Cooldown: 20 hours.$
1. You can assign idle commanders in your city to lead auto peacekeeping troops, which will seek out and automatically attack Barbarian Troops.
2. Manually giving an order to an auto peacekeeping troop will cause it to exit auto peacekeeping mode.
3. If you exit the game, your troops will immediately exit auto peacekeeping mode.
4. Your auto peacekeeping troops will attempt to focus fire on Barbarian Troops when possible.
5. Auto peacekeeping will not target barbarian troops currently or about to become engaged in combat with other governors.
Warrior's HymnW
As the dying sun swallows cries of war, and the ceaseless clang of weapons becomes backdrop to a bloodstained twilight sky, warriors far from home offer up their loyalty amidst roaring battlegrounds, one after another. The golden sun has set, now, its heat scorching these plains, with distant fires tracing the shadowy shapes of mountains. Would heaven and earth rage at the sight if they could, shedding tears of golden blood?
Until moonlight spills and the humming breeze sweeps away the ash of day, all that remains is a fading lyre tune, a hymn of victory and a requiem for the warrior's soul.

RULES
Pioneer Rules&
1. What is the Pioneer Server?
The Pioneer Server is a limited-time test server. New gameplay and story content will be selectively available in the Pioneer Server for governors to experience.
You must meet certain conditions to access the server. In addition, you must register on the event page in advance for a chance to be selected. Registration is currently open to all Android, iOS, and PC users.
2. How do I get selected for the Pioneer Server?
The Pioneer Server will be <color=#c88800>periodically available</color> for various game content. You will be notified beforehand via announcements, after which you may register on the event page for a chance to be selected.
3. What rewards can I get from the Pioneer Server?
You <color=#c88800>won't be able to make purchases</color> while in the Pioneer Server. However, we've prepared various rewards for selected governors to improve the testing experience.
You may freely <color=#c88800>select a character from your official game account</color> to bind to the Pioneer Server. Whenever you complete quests in the server, your bound character will receive corresponding rewards via mail. Please refer to information on Pioneer experience quests for details.
For governors who actively provide constructive feedback in official channels and promptly report critical game issues, we will also issue rewards to their official account character.
4. What kind of new content is available in the Pioneer Server?
In the Pioneer Server, you can experience new gameplay, stories, and features in advance.
Please note that content shown in the Pioneer Server may differ from finalized versions in the official game.
5. What else should I know about the Pioneer Server?
The Pioneer Server is a <color=#c88800>data-clearing test server</color>. We will periodically clear character data, perform technical maintenance and updates, and close the server after tests are complete.
The Pioneer Server and official game server do not share data. Spending Gems or other items in the Pioneer Server <color=#c88800>will not affect your official game account</color>.
In the Pioneer Server, we may provide you with a test character, or a character identical to your official character. No matter what kind of server character is provided, <color=#c88800>it will not affect your official account character</color>.
You can only register <color=#c88800>one character</color> from your account for the Pioneer Server each time.
<color=#c88800>The Pioneer Server's goal is to locate critical game issues and feedback</color>. Issues such as lost progress, disconnections, or item purchase errors are within normal testing expectations and will not be addressed. <color=#c88800>Related complaints will similarly be dismissed for the time being</color>. We thank you for your understanding in this regard.
Any third-party cheating tools are forbidden in the Pioneer Server. Depending on the severity of the offense, violators will have their accounts suspended for various periods of time. Official game accounts are subject to the same penalties along with public disclosure.
REGISTER
Duration: {p1}
Pioneer Experience Registration
Your character must meet the following requirements to register for the Pioneer experience:
1. Your city hall reached level 25.
2. You logged in with your character for 3 consecutive days prior to registration, including the day of.
3. Your power exceeds 20,000,000.
4. You character is more than 2 months old.
This Pioneer experience will take place in a separate test server and will not affect normal game activity.
When the test begins, we will provide participating governors with a test character, along with additional items and resources to facilitate testing.
During the test, you may submit feedback, suggestions, or guides based on your experience to help us make appropriate game adjustments.

1. Message Channel: Select the feedback option in the message channel to send us text and images.
2. Surveys: After completing the Pioneer experience, you may fill out our surveys for additional rewards.

It is said that the world shall one day meet its end in flames. The fire giant will call forth the fury of the earth, burning all life in its wake along with the order of the gods. And so the wise elders built their forts by the abyssal crevice, where the stone warrior stands guard night and day. Millennia have passed and the prophecy is long forgotten, and yet the stone warrior stands tall, surrounded by undying flames, forever awaiting that fateful day.

Steam Patrolm
As the winds carry hints of lightning and thunder, a giant silhouette seems to emerge from the cloudy mist.
In these storm-shrouded dark skies, the very airs are signs of the wrath of heaven. And yet, this bold challenger dives ahead, an azure-gold shark that ruptures the nebulous haze. With a razor sharp bite and voltaic breaths, the lord of the deep now covets higher ground.
The vessel's wings of steel are a harbor for hope, a blueprint of the future traced through rising steam. If the deities above could see, perhaps they would also lend a knowing gaze.
How far should one fly, to hear the whispers of the gods?
